# Skills · E · part 8

814 entries, earnings-deep-dive to easy-llm-wiki. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **earnings-deep-dive**: Analyze a public company after earnings using reported financials, company-native KPIs, management commentary, SEC filings, and investor-relations materials. Use for "earnings review", "analyze the latest quarter", "deep dive on results", or "what changed after earnings". Use earnings-flash for a fast first read. ([signals-ai/claude-plugin](https://github.com/signals-ai/claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/earnings-deep-dive))
- **earnings-delta**: Compare the latest quarter against the prior quarter and the year-ago quarter to isolate what actually changed. Use when the user asks what changed this quarter, to analyze an earnings print, quarter-over-quarter deltas, or says "/earnings-delta". ([johnhefreeman-cell/Meridian-Freeman-Capital](https://github.com/johnhefreeman-cell/Meridian-Freeman-Capital/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/earnings-delta))
- **earnings-digest**: Generates an analyst-grade earnings digest covering results vs. consensus, guidance, management commentary, Q&A signals, and market reaction. Trigger: earnings recap, post-print summary, quarter review, or "how did [company] do this quarter?" Do NOT use before the print (earnings-preview) or for multi-quarter consensus revision trends (earnings-revisions). ([Distilla-AI/distilla-skills](https://github.com/Distilla-AI/distilla-skills/tree/HEAD/earnings-digest) · ★11)
